The History of Italy, Vol. 4 (Francesco Guicciardini (Austin Parke Goddard, trans.), 1561)
==========================================================
Guicciardini's Storia d'Italia (written 1537-40, first printed 1561) - the first 'modern' analytical history; the Italian wars as the tragedy of a divided peninsula. Goddard's English translation (1753-61, 10 vols.). Vol. 4 of 10.

Chevalier AUSTIN PaRKE GoDDARD,

Enight of the Military Order of St. Stephen.

Containing the — and EicuTt Books
of the Hiſtory.

Boxes; one green ſor receiving the Balls of thoſe who are

pne of the three Boxes he pleaſes unperceived.

its Liberty at the ſame Time, and there
is not a Citizen that is born or dies in
Venice, but is born and dies free. Nor
was this Liberty ever interrupted or di-

civil Concord, the Principles of which
are ſo firmly rooted in the Minds of our
People, that as ſoon as they enter into
our Senate, and into our public Debates,
they lay aſide all private Contentions and
Animoſities. For the ſame Reaſon has
our Form of Government, which is a
Mixture of all that is moſt commendable

and compoſed with ſuch Harmony as to

perfectly anſwerable to itſelf, laſted for

and enjoined them for Penance to viſit the
Seven Churches. Having thus obtained
Abfolution they entered the Church of
St. Peter, being introduced by the chief
Penitentiary. From hence they were
honourably accompanied, not as Perſons
